akebox[8.21536pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.21536pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.98846pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.5632pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{,z}}}}{\pi^{{{zz}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.73732pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.73732pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.85535pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75381pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[8.21536pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.21536pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.98846pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.5632pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{,z}}}}\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}&\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}q^{x}+2q^{x}\partial_{x}V\\[4.0pt]
&\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}q^{y}+q^{y}\partial_{x}V+q^{x}\partial_{y}V\\[4.0pt]
&\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}q^{z}+q^{z}\partial_{x}V+q^{x}\partial_{z}V\end{pmatrix}\quad(xi)\,,

or,
-\bm{\partial}\cdot\bm{p}=\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}{\bm{q}}+(\partial_{x}V){\bm{q}}+q^{x}\bm{\partial}V
.

We express the above using
\bm{A}
and calculate the curl of the vorticity transport equation:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\bm{\partial}\times(\bm{\partial}\times\bm{A})

\displaystyle=\bm{\partial}\times\left(\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\bm{\Omega}+\bm{\Omega}(\bm{\partial}\cdot\bm{V})-(\bm{\Omega}\cdot\bm{\partial})\bm{V}\right)\,,

\displaystyle\Leftrightarrow\frac{1}{8\pi G}\begin{pmatrix}&-\partial^{2}_{y}A-\partial^{2}_{z}A\\[4.0pt]
&\partial_{x}(\partial_{y}A)\\[4.0pt]
&\partial_{x}(\partial_{z}A)\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}&\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}q^{x}-\frac{5}{2}q^{y}\partial_{y}V-\frac{5}{2}q^{z}\partial_{z}V+q^{x}\partial_{x}V\\[4.0pt]
&\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}q^{y}+2q^{y}\partial_{x}V+\frac{1}{2}q^{y}\partial_{y}V+\frac{1}{16\pi G}\partial_{y}V\partial_{x}^{2}V\\[4.0pt]
&\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}q^{z}+2q^{z}\partial_{x}V+\frac{1}{2}q^{z}\partial_{z}V+\frac{1}{16\pi G}\partial_{z}V\partial_{x}^{2}V\end{pmatrix}.

Thus,
(xi)
simplifies and reads:

-\bm{\partial}\cdot\bm{p}=\begin{pmatrix}&\frac{-\partial_{y}^{2}A-\partial_{z}^{2}A}{8\pi G}+\frac{5}{2}q^{y}\partial_{y}V+\frac{5}{2}q^{z}\partial_{z}V+q^{x}\partial_{x}V\\[4.0pt]
&\frac{\partial_{x}(\partial_{y}A)}{8\pi G}-q^{y}\partial_{x}V-\frac{1}{2}q^{y}\partial_{z}V+q^{x}\partial_{y}V-\frac{1}{16\pi G}\partial_{y}V\partial_{x}^{2}V\\[4.0pt]
&\frac{\partial_{x}(\partial_{z}A)}{8\pi G}-q^{z}\partial_{x}V-\frac{1}{2}q^{z}\partial_{z}V+q^{x}\partial_{z}V-\frac{1}{16\pi G}\partial_{z}V\partial_{x}^{2}V\end{pmatrix}\quad(xii)\,.

Appendix D
Coordinate acceleration for the Alcubierre solution

We first calculate the coordinate acceleration directly in the Eulerian frame
13
13

13

We use the following derivation laws:
(\tanh{u})^{\prime}=u^{\prime}\sech^{2}(u)\,,\quad(\sech^{2}(u))^{\prime}=-2u^{\prime}\sech^{2}(u)\tanh{(u)}
.
:

\displaystyle A_{S}(t,x^{k})

\displaystyle=\frac{{\mathrm{d}}V_{S}(t,x^{k})}{{\mathrm{d}}t}=\frac{\partial v_{s}(t)}{\partial t}W(r_{S})+v_{S}(t)\frac{{\mathrm{d}}W(r_{S})}{{\mathrm{d}}t}=:a_{S}(t)W(r_{S})+v_{S}(t)\frac{\partial W}{\partial r_{S}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}rs}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\,,

\displaystyle=a_{S}(t)W(r_{S})+v_{S}^{2}(t)g(r_{S})\frac{(x-x_{S}(t))}{r_{S}}[W(r_{S})-1]\,,

where

\displaystyle g(r_{S})=\frac{\partial W}{\partial r_{S}}=\frac{\sigma}{2\tanh{(\sigma R)}}[\sech^{2}{(\sigma(r_{S}+R))}-\sech^{2}{(\sigma(r_{S}-R))}]\,,

with
a_{S}
the acceleration of the warp bubble and we deduce for the gradient of
A_{S}
:

\displaystyle\partial_{x}A_{S}(t,x^{k})

\displaystyle=a_{S}(t)\frac{(x-x_{S})}{r_{S}}g(r_{S})+\Theta^{2}

(152)

\displaystyle+v_{S}^{2}(t)\left[h(r_{S})\frac{(x-x_{S})^{2}}{r_{S}^{2}}(W(r_{S})-1)+g(r_{S})\left(\frac{r_{S}^{2}-(x-x_{S})^{2}}{r_{S}^{3}}(W(r_{S})-1)\right)\right],

\displaystyle\partial_{y}A_{S}(t,x^{k})

\displaystyle=a_{S}(t)\frac{y}{r_{S}}g(r_{S})+\frac{\Theta^{2}y}{x-x_{S}}

\displaystyle+v_{S}^{2}(t)\left[h(r_{S})\frac{(x-x_{S})y}{r_{S}^{2}}(W(r_{S})-1)+g(r_{S})\left(-\frac{(x-x_{S})y}{r_{S}^{3}}(W(r_{S})-1)\right)\right],

\displaystyle\partial_{z}A_{S}(t,x^{k})

\displaystyle=a_{S}(t)\frac{z}{r_{S}}g(r_{S})+\frac{\Theta^{2}z}{(x-x_{S})}

\displaystyle+v_{S}^{2}(t)\left[h(r_{S})\frac{(x-x_{S})z}{r_{S}^{2}}(W(r_{S})-1)+g(r_{S})\left(-\frac{(x-x_{S})z}{r_{S}^{3}}(W(r_{S})-1)\right)\right],

\displaystyle\textrm{where}\quad h(r_{S})

\displaystyle=\frac{\partial^{2}W}{\partial r_{S}^{2}}=\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\tanh{(\sigma R)}}[\sech^{2}{(\sigma(r_{S}+R))}\tanh{(\sigma(r_{S}+R))}

\displaystyle-\sech^{2}{(\sigma(r_{S}-R))}\tanh{(\sigma(r_{S}-R))}]\ .

To understand how the acceleration field changes when we move the warp, we also calculate the following derivative and illustrate it in figure
7
:

\partial_{r_{S}}A_{S}(t,x^{k})=\frac{x-x_{S}}{r_{S}}\partial_{x}A_{S}(t,x^{k})+\frac{y}{r_{S}}\partial_{y}A_{S}(t,x^{k})+\frac{z}{r_{S}}\partial_{z}A_{S}(t,x^{k})\,.

(153)

Figure 7:
The norm of the
r_{S}
-derivative of the coordinate acceleration field. Two views are proposed, one facing us (direction of movement is along x from left to right), and another from above.

Finally, we briefly show that the total coordinate acceleration averaged over the warp field implies that the warp field itself does not have a net contribution to acceleration for vanishing external acceleration
a_{S}(t)
.
We have:

A_{S}=\frac{dV_{S}}{dt}=\partial_{t}V_{S}+V_{S}\partial_{x}V_{S}=a_{S}(t)W(r_{S})+v_{S}(t)\frac{\partial W}{\partial r_{S}}\frac{dr_{S}}{dt}\,,

(154)

and define

\bm{q}=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}q_{x}=x-x_{S}(t)\\
q_{y}=y\\
q_{z}=z\\
\end{array}\right.\Rightarrow r_{S}=\sqrt{q_{x}^{2}+q_{y}^{2}+q_{z}^{2}}\ ,\ \text{so}\quad V_{S}(\bm{q},t)=v_{S}(t)W(r_{S})\ .

(155)

Taking spherical coordinates,
{\mathrm{d}}^{3}q=r_{S}^{2}\sin{\theta}\,{\mathrm{d}}\theta\,{\mathrm{d}}\phi\,{\mathrm{d}}r_{S}
, the volume average yields:

\langle A\rangle_{\mathbb{S}}=\frac{1}{\textrm{Vol}(\mathbb{S})}\int_{\mathbb{S}}A{\mathrm{d}}^{3}q=\frac{a_{S}(t)}{\textrm{Vol}(\mathbb{S})}\int_{\mathbb{S}}W(r_{S})r_{S}^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}^{3}q=\frac{3a_{S}(t)}{R^{3}}\int_{0}^{R}W(r_{S})r_{S}^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}r_{S}\ .

(156)

This means that for
a_{S}(t)=0
the average of the coordinate acceleration vanishes.

Appendix E
Stress anisotropic scalar for inertial motion

We take the
\mathchoice{p^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{p^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{p^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{p^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}
and
p
expressions from Appendix
C
and deduce the anisotropic stress components for the second example solution (
idem

\partial_{i}A=0
). With this we can calculate the stress anisotropic scalar
\Pi^{2}
:

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\pi^{{{x}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{x}}}}{\pi^{{{x}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{x}}}}{\pi^{{{x}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{x}}}}{\pi^{{{x}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{x}}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{8\pi G}(-3\Omega^{2}+\Lambda)-p\quad\Rightarrow\quad\mathchoice{\pi^{{{x}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{x}}}}{\pi^{{{x}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{x}}}}{\pi^{{{x}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{x}}}}{\pi^{{{x}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{x}}}}=\frac{1}{8\pi G}(-2\Omega^{2})\,,

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\pi^{{{y}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{y}}}}{\pi^{{{y}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{y}}}}{\pi^{{{y}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{y}}}}{\pi^{{{y}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{y}}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{8\pi G}\left[\Lambda+\Omega^{2}-2(\Omega^{z})^{2}\right]-p\quad\Rightarrow\quad\mathchoice{\pi^{{{y}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{y}}}}{\pi^{{{y}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{y}}}}{\pi^{{{y}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{y}}}}{\pi^{{{y}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{y}}}}=\frac{1}{8\pi G}\left[2\Omega^{2}-2(\Omega^{z})^{2}\right]\,,

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\pi^{{{z}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{z}}}}{\pi^{{{z}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{z}}}}{\pi^{{{z}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{z}}}}{\pi^{{{z}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{z}}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{8\pi G}\left[\Lambda+\Omega^{2}-2(-\Omega^{y})^{2}\right]-p\quad\Rightarrow\quad\mathchoice{\pi^{{{z}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{z}}}}{\pi^{{{z}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{z}}}}{\pi^{{{z}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{z}}}}{\pi^{{{z}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{z}}}}=\frac{1}{8\pi G}\left[2\Omega^{2}-2(\Omega^{y})^{2}\right]\,.

We have
\pi^{\mu\nu}=h^{\mu\alpha}h^{\nu\beta}\pi_{\alpha\beta}
and we see immediately that the
0
-components are null. Furthermore,
\mathchoice{\pi^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\pi^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\pi^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\pi^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}=h^{\mu\alpha}\pi_{\alpha\nu}
. We only have spatial components and
\pi^{ij}=\mathchoice{\pi^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{\pi^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{\pi^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{\pi^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}=\pi_{ij}
, so that we can simplify the scalar expression as follows:

\Pi^{2}=\frac{1}{2}\pi^{ij}\pi_{ij}=\frac{1}{2}(\pi_{xx}^{2}+\pi_{yy}^{2}+\pi_{zz}^{2}+2\pi_{xy}^{2}+2\pi_{xz}^{2}+2\pi_{yz}^{2})\,.

(157)

Now we replace the pressure,
p=\frac{1}{8\pi G}(-\Omega^{2}+\Lambda)
, and simplify:

\displaystyle\pi_{xx}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(8\pi G)^{2}}(4\Omega^{4})\,,

\displaystyle\pi_{yy}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(8\pi G)^{2}}\left[4\Omega^{4}-8\Omega^{2}(\Omega^{z})^{2}+4(\Omega^{z})^{4}\right]\,,

\displaystyle\pi_{zz}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(8\pi G)^{2}}\left[4\Omega^{4}-8\Omega^{2}(\Omega^{y})^{2}+4(\Omega^{y})^{4}\right]\,,

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\quad

\displaystyle\pi_{xx}^{2}+\pi_{yy}^{2}+\pi_{zz}^{2}=\frac{1}{(8\pi G)^{2}}\left[8\Omega^{2}-8(\Omega^{z})^{2}(\Omega^{y})^{2}\right]\,,

\pi_{xy}^{2}=\frac{1}{(8\pi G)^{2}}(\Omega^{z})^{2}(\partial_{x}V)^{2},\ \pi_{xz}^{2}=\frac{1}{(8\pi G)^{2}}(\Omega^{y})^{2}(\partial_{x}V)^{2},\ \pi_{yz}^{2}=\frac{2}{(8\pi G)^{2}}(\Omega^{y})^{2}(\Omega^{z})^{2},

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\quad 2\pi_{xy}^{2}+2\pi_{xz}^{2}+2\pi_{yz}^{2}=\frac{1}{(8\pi G)^{2}}\left[2\Omega^{2}\Theta^{2}+8(\Omega^{z})^{2}(\Omega^{y})^{2}\right]\,,

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\quad\pi_{xx}^{2}+\pi_{yy}^{2}+\pi_{zz}^{2}+2\pi_{xy}^{2}+2\pi_{xz}^{2}+2\pi_{yz}^{2}=\frac{1}{(8\pi G)^{2}}\left(8\Omega^{4}+2\Omega^{2}\Theta^{2}\right)\,.

We finally obtain for the stress anisotropic scalar:

\Pi^{2}=\frac{1}{(8\pi G)^{2}}(4\Omega^{4}+\Omega^{2}\Theta^{2})\,.

(158)
